\ \times\sum_{k=0}^{n}\binom{n}{k}^{2}|f^{(k)}_{\tilde{\ell}\tilde{m}}|^{2}(\ell^{\prime}+1)^{4}(n-k+1)^{2}|g^{(n-k)}_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}|^{2}.

We introduce the summation index
j=n-k
, we rearrange the summation and separate the cases
\tilde{\ell}\leq\ell
and
\tilde{\ell}>\ell
to obtain:

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\frac{(d_{0}x_{0})^{2n}}{(\max\{\ell+1,n+1\})^{2n}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(gf)_{\ell}^{(n)}|^{2}\,d\upsigma\\
\leq C\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\tilde{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{\tilde{m}=-\tilde{\ell}}^{\tilde{\ell}}\frac{(d_{0}x_{0})^{2k}}{(\max\{\tilde{\ell}+1,k+1\})^{2k}}|f_{\tilde{\ell}\tilde{m}}^{(k)}|^{2}\\
\times\Biggl[\sum_{\ell^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{m^{\prime}=-\ell^{\prime}}^{\ell^{\prime}}\sum_{\ell=|\tilde{\ell}-\ell^{\prime}|}^{\tilde{\ell}+\ell^{\prime}}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(d_{0}x_{0})^{2j}\frac{(\max\{\tilde{\ell}+1,k+1\})^{2k}}{(\max\{\ell+1,k+j+1\})^{2(k+j)}}\binom{k+j}{k}^{2}\\
\times(j+1)^{2}(\ell^{\prime}+1)^{4}|g_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}^{(j)}|^{2}\Biggr]

We will now bound the terms inside the square brackets uniformly in
k
and
\tilde{\ell}
.

Note first of all that

(k+j+1)^{-2j}\binom{k+j}{k}^{2}=\frac{1}{j!^{2}}(k+j+1)^{-2j}(k+j)^{2}(k+j-1)^{2}\ldots(k+1)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{j!^{2}}.

Now suppose
\tilde{\ell}+1\leq k+1
. Then

\left(\frac{\max\{\tilde{\ell}+1,k+1\}}{\max\{\ell+1,k+j+1\}}\right)^{2k}\leq\left(\frac{k+1}{k+j+1}\right)^{2k}\leq 1.

Suppose
\tilde{\ell}+1>k+1
. Then

\left(\frac{\max\{\tilde{\ell}+1,k+1\}}{\max\{\ell+1,k+j+1\}}\right)^{2k}\leq\left(\frac{\tilde{\ell}+1}{\max\{\ell+1,k+1\}}\right)^{2k}=e^{2k\log\left(\frac{\tilde{\ell}+1}{\max\{\ell+1,k+1\}}\right)}.

If
\tilde{\ell}\leq\ell
, then the right-hand side can be bounded by 1. Suppose therefore that
\tilde{\ell}>\ell
. We have that
\tilde{\ell}\leq\ell^{\prime}+\ell
, so we can bound further:

e^{2k\log\left(\frac{\tilde{\ell}+1}{\max\{\ell+1,k+1\}}\right)}\leq e^{2k\log\left(1+\frac{\ell^{\prime}}{k+1}\right)}\leq e^{2\ell^{\prime}\frac{k+1}{\ell^{\prime}}\log\left(1+\frac{\ell^{\prime}}{k+1}\right)}\leq e^{2\ell^{\prime}},

where we obtained the final inequality by using that
x^{-1}\log(1+x)\leq 1
for
x\geq 0
.

We can therefore infer that:

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\frac{(d_{0}x_{0})^{2n}}{(\max\{\ell+1,n+1\})^{2n}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(gf)_{\ell}^{(n)}|^{2}\,d\upsigma\\
\leq C\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\tilde{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{\tilde{m}=-\tilde{\ell}}^{\tilde{\ell}}\frac{(d_{0}x_{0})^{2k}}{(\max\{\tilde{\ell}+1,k+1\})^{2k}}|f_{\tilde{\ell}\tilde{m}}^{(k)}|^{2}\\
\times\Biggl[\sum_{\ell^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{m^{\prime}=-\ell^{\prime}}^{m=\ell^{\prime}}\sum_{\ell=|\tilde{\ell}-\ell^{\prime}|}^{\tilde{\ell}+\ell^{\prime}}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(\ell^{\prime}+1)^{4}e^{2\ell^{\prime}}\frac{(j+1)^{2}(d_{0}x_{0})^{2j}}{j!^{2}}|g_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}^{(j)}|^{2}\Biggr]

Then (
C.1
) follows from the observation that
\sum_{\ell=|\tilde{\ell}-\ell^{\prime}|}^{\tilde{\ell}+\ell^{\prime}}1=\tilde{\ell}+\ell^{\prime}-|\tilde{\ell}-\ell^{\prime}|\leq 2\ell^{\prime}
.

The estimate (
C.2
) follows in an analogous manner, where the above computations become simplified since the relevant norms depend only a finite number of derivatives, and we use that for
\tilde{\ell}\leq\ell
,
(\ell(\ell+1)+1)^{-w}\leq(\tilde{\ell}(\tilde{\ell}+1)+1)^{-w}
and for
\tilde{\ell}>\ell
(in which case
\tilde{\ell}<\ell+\ell^{\prime}
):

(C.3)

(\ell(\ell+1)+1)^{-w}=(\tilde{\ell}(\tilde{\ell}+1)+1)^{-w}\left(\frac{\tilde{\ell}(\tilde{\ell}+1)+1}{\ell(\ell+1)+1}\right)^{w}\leq(\tilde{\ell}(\tilde{\ell}+1)+1)^{-w}\left(\frac{(\ell+\ell^{\prime})(\ell+\ell^{\prime}+1)}{\ell(\ell+1)+1}\right)^{w}\\
=(\tilde{\ell}(\tilde{\ell}+1)+1)^{-w}\left(1+\frac{\ell^{\prime}(\ell^{\prime}+1)}{\ell(\ell+1)+1}+\frac{2\ell\ell^{\prime}}{\ell(\ell+1)+1}\right)^{w}\\
\leq(\tilde{\ell}(\tilde{\ell}+1)+1)^{-w}\left(1+\ell^{\prime}(\ell^{\prime}+1)\right)^{w}.

∎

Lemma C.3
.

Ther exists a constants
C,\beta>0
such that for all appropriately regular functions
f,g;[0,x_{0}]\times\mathbb{S}^{2}\to\mathbb{C}
, we can estimate:

(C.4)

\|gf\|^{2}_{G_{\sigma,R_{\infty}}}\leq C\|f\|^{2}_{G_{\sigma,R_{\infty}}}\left[\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\frac{e^{5(\ell^{\prime}+j)}}{j!^{4}}\|\partial_{x}^{j}g_{\ell^{\prime}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}([0,x_{0}]\times\mathbb{S}^{2})}\right].

Proof.

We can express:

\|gf\|^{2}_{G_{\sigma,R_{\infty}}}=\sum_{\ell\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{n=0}^{\ell+l-1}\frac{(4\sigma)^{2n}(\ell-(n+1))!^{2}}{(\ell+n+1)!^{2}}\int_{0}^{x_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|\partial_{x}^{n}(gf)_{\ell}|^{2}\,d\upsigma dx\\
+(\mathfrak{l}+1)^{3}\sum_{n=\ell+l}^{\infty}\frac{\sigma^{2n}}{n!^{2}(n+1)!^{2}}\int_{0}^{x_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|\partial_{x}^{n}(gf)_{\ell}|^{2}\,d\upsigma dx.

We then proceed as in the proof of Lemma
C.2
by decomposing
g
and
f
into spherical harmonics:
g=\sum_{\ell^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{m=-\ell^{\prime}}^{\ell^{\prime}}g_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}Y_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}
,
f=\sum_{\tilde{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{\tilde{m}=-\tilde{\ell}}^{\tilde{\ell}}f_{\tilde{\ell}\tilde{m}}Y_{\tilde{\ell}\tilde{m}}
and using that

|(gf)_{\ell}^{(n)}|^{2}\leq\frac{\pi^{2}}{6}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\binom{n}{k}^{2}(n-k+1)^{2}|(g^{(n-k)}f^{(k)})_{\ell}|^{2}.

We then obtain:

\|gf\|^{2}_{G_{\sigma,R_{\infty}}}\leq C\sum_{\ell\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{\{\ell^{\prime},\tilde{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}\,|\,|\tilde{\ell}-\ell^{\prime}|\leq\ell\leq\ell+\ell^{\prime}\}}\sum_{\tilde{m}=-\tilde{\ell}}^{\tilde{\ell}}\sum_{m=-\ell^{\prime}}^{\ell^{\prime}}\sum_{n=0}^{\ell-1}\frac{(4\sigma)^{2n}(\ell-(n+1))!^{2}}{(\ell+n+1)!^{2}}\\
\times\int_{0}^{x_{0}}|f_{\tilde{\ell}\tilde{m}}^{(k)}|^{2}\binom{n}{k}^{2}(n-k+1)^{2}\|Y_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}^{2}|g_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}^{(n-k)}|^{2}\,d\upsigma dx\\
+\left(\frac{4\sigma}{d_{0}x_{0}}\right)^{-2\ell}(\ell+1)^{3}\sum_{n=\ell}^{\infty}\frac{\sigma^{2n}}{n!^{2}(n+1)!^{2}}\int_{0}^{x_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|f_{\tilde{\ell}\tilde{m}}^{(k)}|^{2}\binom{n}{k}^{2}(n-k+1)^{2}\|Y_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}^{2}|g_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}^{(n-k)}|^{2}\,d\upsigma dx.

By introducing the summation index
j=n-k
and taking
\sup_{x\in[0,x_{0}]}|g^{(n-k)}_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}|^{2}(x)
outside of the
x
-integral, we can rewrite the right-hand side above to obtain:

\|gf\|^{2}_{G_{\sigma,R_{\infty}}}\leq C\sum_{\tilde{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{\tilde{m}=-\tilde{\ell}}^{\tilde{\ell}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tilde{\ell}-1}\frac{(4\sigma)^{2k}(\tilde{\ell}-(k+1))!^{2}}{(\tilde{\ell}+k+1)!^{2}}\\
\times\int_{0}^{x_{0}}|f^{(k)}_{\tilde{\ell}\tilde{m}}|^{2}\,dx\\
\times\Biggl[\sum_{\ell^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{\ell=|\tilde{\ell}-\ell^{\prime}|}^{\tilde{\ell}+\ell^{\prime}}\sum_{m=-\ell^{\prime}}^{\ell^{\prime}}\Biggl(\sum_{j=0}^{\ell-1-k}(4\sigma)^{2j}\underbrace{\frac{(\tilde{\ell}+k+1)!^{2}({\ell}-(k+j+1))!^{2}}{({\ell}+k+j+1)!^{2}(\tilde{\ell}-(k+1))!^{2}}\frac{(k+j)!^{2}}{k!^{2}j!^{2}}}_{=:J_{1}}(j+1)^{2}\|g^{(j)}_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}Y_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\\
+\sum_{j=\ell-k}^{\infty}4^{-2k}(4\sigma)^{2j}(\ell+1)^{3}\underbrace{\frac{(\tilde{\ell}+k+1)!^{2}}{(\tilde{\ell}-(k+1))!^{2}(k+j+1)!^{2}(k+j)!^{2}}\frac{(k+j)!^{2}}{k!^{2}j!^{2}}}_{=:J_{2}}(j+1)^{2}\|g^{(j)}_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}Y_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\Biggr)\Biggr]\\
+\sum_{k=\tilde{\ell}+l}^{\infty}\frac{\sigma^{2k}(\tilde{\ell}+1)^{3}}{(k+1)!^{2}k!^{2}}\int_{0}^{x_{0}}|f^{(k)}_{\tilde{\ell}\tilde{m}}|^{2}\,dx\Biggl[\sum_{\ell^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{\ell=|\tilde{\ell}-\ell^{\prime}|}^{\tilde{\ell}+\ell^{\prime}}\sum_{m=-\ell^{\prime}}^{\ell^{\prime}}\Biggl(\sum_{j=0}^{\min\{\ell-1-k,0\}}4^{2k}(4\sigma)^{2j}(\tilde{\ell}+1)^{-3}\\
\times\underbrace{\frac{(k+1)!^{2}k!^{2}({\ell}-(k+j+1))!^{2}}{({\ell}+k+j+1)!^{2}}\frac{(k+j)!^{2}}{k!^{2}j!^{2}}}_{=:J_{3}}(j+1)^{2}\|g^{(j)}_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}Y_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\\
+\sum_{j=\max\{\ell+l-k,0\}}^{\infty}\sigma^{j}\left(\frac{\ell+1}{\tilde{\ell}+1}\right)^{3}\underbrace{\frac{(k+1)!^{2}k!^{2}}{(k+j+1)!^{2}(k+j)!^{2}}\frac{(k+j)!^{2}}{k!^{2}j!^{2}}}_{=:J_{4}}(j+1)^{2}\|g^{(j)}_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}Y_{\ell^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\Biggr)\Biggr].

We will simplify the terms inside square brackets.
We will first estimate
J_{1}
. We have that:

J_{1}=\frac{(\tilde{\ell}+k+1)^{2}(\tilde{\ell}+k)^{2}\ldots(\tilde{\ell}-k)^{2}}{(\ell+k+j+1)^{2}\ldots(\ell+k+j)^{2}\ldots(\ell-k-j)^{2}}\frac{(k+j)!^{2}}{k!^{2}j!^{2}}

Suppose that
\tilde{\ell}\leq\ell
. Then, using that
\ell\geq k+j+1
, we estimate

J_{1}\leq\frac{1}{(\ell+k+j+1)^{2}\ldots(\ell+k+1)^{2}(\ell-k-1)^{2}\ldots(\ell-k-j)^{2}}\frac{(k+j)!^{2}}{k!^{2}j!^{2}}\\
\leq\frac{(k+j)^{2}(k+j-1)^{2}\ldots(k+1)^{2}}{(2k+j+2+j)^{2}\ldots(2k+j+2)^{2}}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}\leq\frac{1}{j!^{4}}.

Now suppose that
\tilde{\ell}\geq\ell+1
. Then we estimate using that
k+j\leq\ell-1

J_{1}\leq\frac{(\tilde{\ell}+k+1)^{2}\ldots(\tilde{\ell}-k)^{2}}{(\ell+k+1)^{2}\ldots(\ell-k)^{2}}\\
\times\frac{1}{(\ell+k+j+1)^{2}\ldots(\ell+k+2)^{2}(\ell-k-1)^{2}\ldots(\ell-k-j)^{2}}\frac{(k+j)!^{2}}{k!^{2}j!^{2}}\\
\leq\frac{(\tilde{\ell}+k+1)^{2}\ldots(\ell+k+2)^{2}}{(\tilde{\ell}-k-1)^{2}\ldots(\ell-k)^{2}}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}\\
\leq\frac{(2\ell+1+(\tilde{\ell}-\ell-1))^{2}\ldots(2\ell+1)^{2}}{(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)!^{2}}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}\\
\leq Ce^{2(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)}(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)^{-2(\tilde{\ell}-\ell-1)-3}(2\ell+l+1+(\tilde{\ell}-\ell-1))^{2(\tilde{\ell}-\ell-1)}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}\\
\leq Ce^{2(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)}(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)^{-3}\left(1+\frac{2\ell+1}{\tilde{\ell}-\ell}\right)^{2(\tilde{\ell}-\ell-1)}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}

Suppose now that
\tilde{\ell}-\ell\geq\frac{1}{2}(2\ell+1)
. Then we can further estimate:

J_{1}\leq Ce^{2(1+\log\left(\frac{3}{2}\right))(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)}(\tilde{\ell}-\ell)^{-3}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}\leq Ce^{2(1+\log\left(\frac{3}{2}\right))\ell^{\prime}}\ell^{-3}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}.

If instead
\tilde{\ell}-\ell<\frac{1}{2}(2\ell+1)
, then
\tilde{\ell}\leq 2\ell+\frac{1}{2}
and we estimate:

J_{1}\leq\frac{(\tilde{\ell}+k+1)^{2}\ldots(\tilde{\ell}-k)^{2}}{(\ell+k+1)^{2}\ldots(\ell-k)^{2}}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}\leq 4^{2k+1}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}.

We now turn to
J_{2}
.

Note first that
k\geq\ell-j\geq\tilde{\ell}-(\ell^{\prime}+j)
, so
\tilde{\ell}\leq k+(\ell^{\prime}+j)
. Hence,

\frac{(\tilde{\ell}+k+1)!^{2}}{(\tilde{\ell}-(k+1))!^{2}(k+1)!^{4}}\leq\frac{(\ell^{\prime}+j-1+2(k+1))!^{2}}{(\ell^{\prime}+j-1)!^{2}(k+1)!^{4}}

Via Stirling’s approximation, we can further estimate

\frac{(\ell^{\prime}+j-1+2(k+1))!^{2}}{(\ell^{\prime}+j-1)!^{2}(k+1)!^{4}}\leq C\left(1+\frac{2(k+1)}{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}\right)^{2(\ell^{\prime}+j-1)+1}\left(2+\frac{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}{k+1}\right)^{4(k+1)}\\
\leq Ce^{2(k+1)\frac{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}{2(k+1)}\log\left(1+\frac{2(k+1)}{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}\right)}e^{4(\ell^{\prime}+j-1)\frac{k+1}{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}\log\left(2+\frac{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}{k+1}\right)}\\
\leq Ce^{2(k+1)\frac{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}{2(k+1)}\log\left(1+\frac{2(k+1)}{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}\right)}e^{4\log\left(1+\ell^{\prime}+j\right)},

where we obtained the final inequality by using that
x^{-1}\log(2+x)
is strictly decreasing. Suppose that
2(k+1)\geq\ell^{\prime}+j-1
. Then, by the decreasing property of
x^{-1}\log(1+x)
, we have that

e^{2(k+1)\frac{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}{2(k+1)}\log\left(1+\frac{2(k+1)}{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}\right)}\leq e^{2\log(2)(k+1)}\leq 2^{2(k+1)}.

Suppose now that
2(k+1)<\ell^{\prime}+j-1
. Then we can estimate instead:

e^{2(k+1)\frac{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}{2(k+1)}\log\left(1+\frac{2(k+1)}{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}\right)}=\left(1+\frac{2(k+1)}{\ell^{\prime}+j-1}\right)^{2(\ell^{\prime}+j-1)+1}\leq 2^{2(\ell^{\prime}+j-1)+1}.

Hence, there exists constants
C,\beta>0
such that

\frac{(\tilde{\ell}+k+1)!^{2}}{(\tilde{\ell}-(k+1))!^{2}(k+1)!^{4}}\leq C2^{2\ell^{\prime}+2j}2^{2k}.

From the above, we can therefore estimate
J_{2}
as follows:

4^{-2k}J_{2}\leq 2^{-2k}2^{2\ell^{\prime}+2j}\frac{(k+1)^{2}(k+1)!^{2}}{(k+j+1)!^{2}j!^{2}}\leq C2^{-2k}k^{2}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}

Note also that by
\ell\leq k+j

(\ell+1)^{3}\leq(k+j+1)^{3}\leq(k+1)^{3}\left(1+\frac{j}{k+1}\right)^{3}\leq(k+1)^{3}(j+1)^{3}.

Consider now
J_{3}
. Note that by the Stirling approximation and the fact that
\ell\geq k+j

\frac{(\ell-(k+j+1))!^{2}(k+j+1)!^{4}}{(\ell+(k+j+1))!^{2}}\leq\frac{(k+j+1)!^{4}}{(2(k+j+1))!^{2}}\leq C2^{-4(k+j+1)}(k+j+1),

so

J_{3}\leq C4^{-2(k+j)}\frac{(k+1)!^{2}}{(k+j+1)!^{2}(k+j+1)}\frac{1}{j!^{2}}\leq C4^{-2(k+j)}(k+j+1)^{-1}\frac{1}{j!^{4}}.

We consider
J_{4}
. Note that

J_{4}=\frac{(k+1)!^{2}}{(k+j+1)!^{2}}\frac{1}{j!^{2}}=\frac{1}{(k+j)^{2}\ldots(k+2)!^{2}}\frac{1}{j!^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{j!^{4}}.

Furthermore,

\left(\frac{\ell+1}{\tilde{\ell}+1}\right)^{3}=\left(1+\frac{\ell-\tilde{\ell}}{\tilde{\ell}+1}\right)^{3}\leq C(1+\ell^{\prime})^{3}.

The inequality (
C.4
) follows by combining the above estimates.
∎
